## Refuted (10)

- **X12-6** For a non-admin viewer, the card sends a new subscribe_events request on every hass update, and each one is refused and logged as an error — duplicate of F09-1 (same defect). Path holds: set hass calls _subscribe on every update (card.ts:440); a refused subscribe_events is swallowed without a memo (card.ts:546-547), so _unsub stays unset and the next hass update tries again (card.ts:531); HA Core refuses non-allowlisted event types for non-admins and logs at error level (framework behavior, not verifiable offline). Score it under F09-1.
- **B04-12** Re-importing an export cannot clear a field that was empty at export time — Reproduces, but it is the documented merge rule: docs/guide/automation/import-export.md 'An update only changes the fields the file states. Fields it leaves out keep the value they have.', matching models.merge_update. Only the _strip docstring (transfer.py:330-333, 'costs nothing on the way back in') overstates it for updates. A design choice, not a defect.
- **B07-6** Fixed monthly schedule on day 29-31 drifts permanently to the 28th; a yearly Feb 29 task stays on Feb 28 in leap years — Intended and pinned, not a code defect. test_recurrence_fixed.py:150 asserts 'Day clamps to 28 permanently once a non-leap February is crossed'; :126-142 pins Jan 31 and Feb 29 (interval 12) against single-stepping; recurrence.py:211-222 documents the path dependence and builds the O(1) fast-forward on it; docs/DESIGN.md:20 documents month clamping. The guide sentence at docs/guide/start/concepts.md:11 ('The dates that follow do not move') says a completion does not shift the grid, which holds; it makes no promise about days 29-31. The drift is real (reproduced), but it is a product choice that the user guide does not mention.
- **B14-7** A genuine meter reset or counter wrap discards all usage since the last service instead of carrying it — This is the documented design, not a defect. The guide says 'Home Keeper re-anchors automatically if the reading drops below the baseline after a meter reset' (docs/guide/tasks/sensor-tasks.md:15-16), and evaluate_usage documents the same re-anchor (sensor_tasks.py:350-353, 382-392). Losing the pre-reset progress follows directly from that rule. Carrying progress across a rollover, the way utility_meter does, would be an enhancement.
- **B17-10** Tag-scan completions drop who scanned — Not a defect. No completion surface fills 'who' by itself: the service, the websocket and the panel record it only when the caller passes it (__init__.py:262-351, websocket_api.py:394, 435), the notifier and the to-do list pass none, and store.complete_task fires EVENT_TASK_COMPLETED without a context on every path (store.py:2031-2036). The tag guide (nfc-tags.md) promises the completion and the origin marker, not who scanned. require_tag_scan checks presence, not identity. This is a feature request that applies to every surface.
- **B17-6** Device triggers made on a merged device before 2026.8 stop firing after the heal — Documented trade-off: docs/guide/reference/migration-2026-8.md:31-40 says Home Keeper triggers on a device another integration owns are no longer offered, and that 'an automation built that way must be rebuilt' on the task's entities or the home_keeper_* events. The finder's own notes call this loss a documented trade-off. It also does not stay silent: HA 2026.9 resolves a composite id in a device automation to the split owned by the automation's domain (core device_automation/helpers.py:46-72). After async_prune_orphaned_devices removes Home Keeper's half, validation raises 'has no config entry from domain home_keeper' (helpers.py:133-147), so the automation fails to load with an error.
- **B17-7** A scan completes every task bound to the tag whether or not it is due — Intended behavior. tags.py:39-41 makes the tag many-to-one on purpose, and says 1 tag on the coffee machine can complete 2 tasks in a single scan. The guide says a scan completes the linked task (nfc-tags.md:3-6). Binding 2 tasks with different cadences to one tag is the user's choice, and a separate tag per task gives separate completions. Only enabled tasks match (tags.py:44-48). The docstring example (descale and refill beans have very different cadences) is a poor one, but that is a doc nit.
- **B18-11** Problem-sync passes are not entry-scoped, so one pending across a reload writes to the replaced store — The failure scenario (the mirror stays wrong until the next flip) cannot hold. Every reload runs setup again, and setup calls problem_sync.async_initial_reconcile (__init__.py:842), which re-reads the live sensor states (problem_sync.py:106-176) and applies the same arm/clear to the new store. This happens whether or not the old pass's save reached disk before the new store loaded. The old listeners are removed at unload (entry.async_on_unload, problem_sync.py:78-84), so no new pass starts against the old store. What is left is a narrow race that can fire the triggered or completed event twice for one flip, once from the old pass and once from the new setup's reconcile. Using an entry-scoped background task is still tidier, but this is not a state-loss defect.
- **X01-6** register_companion is open to non-admins and lets any user overwrite a real companion's descriptor and fire companion_connected events — Intended: .amazonq/rules/architecture-and-code.md:36-37 'Usage stays open: tasks (...), profiles, companions, and the dashboard card's reads'; register_companion is deliberately not admin_only in api_surface.py:204. The registry is in-memory and rebuilt on restart (rules:1591-1592); docs_url is restricted to http(s) (companions.py:60-62) and escaped in the panel (panel-settings.ts:1816, 1847). No privilege is gained: a non-admin can already put an arbitrary http(s) link in front of an admin through the open add_task (api_surface.py:166, task_chips url) and fire events carrying caller-chosen text by creating tasks.
- **X02-3** YAML import and export run pure-Python PyYAML on the event loop, up to the 8 MB import cap — duplicate of B03-3 (import and export YAML on the event loop). The import half is also B04-9, which verify-B04 rated CONFIRMED low. The defect is real: transfer_runner.py:62-74 and :39 call parse_document and document_to_yaml on the loop.

## Overflow items (not verified)

- B01: async_merge_split_duplicates rewrites the survivor's device_id in memory but saves only when something was removed (store.py:1327-1344), and fires no task_updated for the rewrite
- B01: async_merge_split_duplicates counts a task that sits in 2 namespace groups twice in `removed` (delete_task on an already-deleted id is a silent no-op)
- B01: delete_asset nulls parent_asset_id on child appliances without firing home_keeper_asset_updated for them (store.py:1474-1476)
- B01: Event payloads embed live references to the stored task's source/managed_by/labels (events.py:39-41), so a Python listener that mutates event.data mutates the store
- B02: delete_archived_completion is open to non-admins (service and ws twin) although archived history lives on the appliance record that the non-admin projection hides; it also silently no-ops for an unknown task_id/ts (store.py:2389-2407).
- B02: store.pause_declarative_companion_tasks says the entity set never changes, but disabling a device-backed task removes its per-task entities; declarative_companion_sync._reconcile_all ignores the pause, so disabling a companion leaves stale entities until the next reload (out of slice).
- B02: SET_TASK_CONSUMABLE_SCHEMA.quantity rejects null while the ws twin accepts it (contract drift, minor).
- B02: Setup failure after entry.runtime_data is set (__init__.py:830) can leave find_coordinator returning a coordinator from a failed setup, since it does not check entry.state (edge case).
- B03: Handlers keep using the coordinator/store captured before an in-flight entry reload (a concurrent card/panel command during another command's async_reload writes to the replaced HomeKeeperStore; if its save lands after the new store's load, the change is lost). Speculative, not specific to this file.
- B03: get_options is open to non-admins and returns the whole options object (notification configs and their mobile_app targets, problem-sensor exclusions); SECURITY.md lists only tasks and profiles as readable.
- B03: A non-admin can add_task with managed_by {deletion_protected: true, config_entry_id: <Home Keeper's own entry id, visible on declarative tasks>}; the panel's deleteTask never sends force, so an admin cannot delete that task from the panel (service with force only).
- B03: set_options websocket takes a raw dict: a bare string for a list option (e.g. problem_sensor_exclude_entities) is stored split into characters by options._normalize, where the service's cv.ensure_list wraps it (admin-only; panel sends lists).
- B03: snooze_task reports an unparseable `until` as code not_allowed with the invalid_task string quoting the raw value, instead of an invalid-date error like complete_task's invalid_completed_at.
- B04: _section filters out non-mapping records and plan_import re-enumerates the rest, so every later record's index/path in problems is shifted (transfer.py:1167-1177, 1006, 1040).
- B04: A non-string top-level key (e.g. `!!binary aGk=: 1`) becomes a Problem whose section/path are bytes, so the websocket/service report cannot be serialized (transfer.py:976-989).
- B04: Task payloads are merged at plan time and written after awaits (asset save, async_reconcile_assets); a completion or watcher trigger on those tasks in that window is overwritten (transfer_runner.py:79-131).
- B04: The name ladder matches reconciler-owned and managed tasks (the matcher is built from all stored tasks), so a hand-written record can update a part-derived task instead of creating its own (transfer.py:996, 757).
- B04: A create whose stated id is unknown skips the external_id step, so it can write a second record with a stored record's external_id; the collision check only looks inside the document (transfer.py:741-742, 1108-1111).
- B04: format: 0 or a negative format is accepted (transfer.py:952-972 checks only > TRANSFER_FORMAT).
- B04: _claim_id keeps non-canonical uuid spellings ({braces}, urn:uuid:, 32-hex, upper case) verbatim as record ids (transfer.py:1255-1257).
- B04: Appliance cost accepts .nan/.inf from a document (assets._normalize_cost has no finite check), which then serialize as null.
- B05: store.update_managed_asset (store.py:1398-1407) writes asset['name'] in place before apply_managed_parts can raise, so a rejected parts list leaves an unsaved, unannounced rename in memory that the next save persists (store.py, outside this slice)
- B05: _merge_parts keeps the stored last_replaced whenever the incoming value is None (assets.py:803-804), so an edit can never clear a wrongly seeded last_replaced; it can only change it
- B05: _merge_documents moves every stored file document before all links on each generic documents write (assets.py:335), so a service write reorders the list
- B05: replace_interval / stock / cost accept a YAML boolean (yes -> 1) and truncate 2.9 -> 2; models.py does the same for interval, so the two models agree
- B06: Archived appliances are included in the report rows and totals with no archived column (store.list_assets() is unfiltered) - intent unclear.
- B06: Two concurrent uploads to the same part with different filenames orphan one blob (old_filename is read once, after streaming, from the admitting request's asset view).
- B06: CSV TOTAL row omits grand_total although the JSON report computes it.
- B06: Integration removal deletes home_keeper/documents but leaves the empty <config>/home_keeper/ directory.
- B07: next_fixed_occurrence compares by wall clock, so during the repeated autumn hour (now 01:15 EST, fold=1) a daily 01:30 task gets today's 01:30 EDT, already past: compute_next_due returns an instant before now and the task is overdue at once (recurrence.py:290,294).
- B07: expand_fixed_occurrences shares one 500-iteration cap between the catch-up loop and the emit loop and truncates silently, e.g. a DAILY task over a calendar.get_events window longer than ~500 days (recurrence.py:328-337).
- B07: calendar.py _next_start/_collect_events ignore a fixed task's stored next_due, so an early completion, skip or snooze still shows the old occurrence on the calendar (outside this slice).
- B07: Known and pinned (xfail strict, test_r4b): a season boundary on 02-29 clamps to Feb 28 in _next_season_start but in_season does not clamp, so the clamped date is out of season 3 years in 4.
- B08: _normalize_for_seconds (models.py:213) has no upper bound; a huge for_seconds overflows timedelta/datetime in sensor_tasks.hold_due_at (:466) and raises OverflowError in the watcher
- B08: infer_recurrence_type (models.py:863) ignores sensor and last_completed: {name, sensor} silently becomes a one-off due now and drops the binding; {name, last_completed} becomes a one-off completed at creation
- B08: validate_managed_by (models.py:804) does not check locked_fields is a list; locked_fields: 'name' becomes set('name') = {'n','a','m','e'} and locks nothing
- B09: recurrence.apply_completion sets last_completed/next_due from any completed_at, so logging an older back-dated completion after a newer one rewinds the schedule (floating task turns overdue) and _stamp_part_replacement moves the wear part's last_replaced backwards and draws a spare (recurrence.py:530-539, store.py:2023-2024)
- B09: reconcile_part_tasks indexes asset['id']/part['id'] without the id guard its siblings have (reconcile.py:623): an id-less wear part in a hand-edited document raises KeyError in async_setup_entry on every start
- B09: Undoing the completion of a buy reminder does not take back the stock _stamp_buy_restock added (no stock_drawn recorded), so an undone 'bought' leaves stock inflated (store.py:2547-2567, 2184)
- B09: store._reject_completion_blocked docstring says the to-do list withholds Done; todo.py lists blocked tasks as NEEDS_ACTION and the tick errors (surfaced, but comment drift)
- B10: Entries of a deleted/cleared profile whose list entity is gone are carried forward forever: needs_pass stays True (every task event reads every list) and the 5-minute sweep always forces a pass (todo_list.py:424-426, 662-663; todo_list_sync.py:150).
- B10: No timeout on todo.get_items / add/update/remove calls (blocking=True): a hung provider call leaves _running True forever and every later sync is folded into _pending until reload (todo_sync_driver.py:215-224, 253-255).
- B10: A held uid-less add is dropped when its task stops matching or its profile is cleared/switched; if the slow add appears later the open line is orphaned forever (todo_list.py:420-438, 462-463).
- B10: A held uid-less entry on the old list after a target switch blocks the add on the new list for the full 20-minute grace, then orphans the late line on the old list (todo_list.py:474-489).
- B10: A one-way frozen entry with a rescheduled task keeps needs_pass True forever (due drift never clears), so every task event does a full read (todo_list.py:517-521, 673-674).
- B10: A failed update_item(status=completed) restores the old entry, then a failed add of the fresh item pops that restored key, losing the old line's tracking (todo_list_sync.py:329-330, 343-344).
- B10: A poke that arrives during the last of the 4 passes is cleared in finally and lost until the next sweep (todo_sync_driver.py:156-172).
- B11: Periodic sweep is gated on tracked being non-empty (shopping_sync.py:107): a transient add_item failure leaves nothing tracked, so the reminder is not retried until an unrelated settle or a list count change (needs_pass would detect it for free).
- B11: Double restock race: _complete_reminders (shopping_sync.py:173-186) completes a task_id planned before _apply's awaits; if the reminder was completed elsewhere meanwhile and the part is still low, store.complete_task has no dormant one-off guard and restocks a second time.
- B11: After an entry reload the old ShoppingListSync can still execute service calls inside _apply (no _stopped check between ops) and then returns without persisting (shopping_sync.py:148-150), so a line it added is untracked and can be orphaned or duplicated by the new instance.
- B11: Completion of a reminder in Home Keeper while a pass is in flight: the 'before reconcile' async_sync() only sets _pending, the reconcile retires the reminder, and the line is removed instead of ticked off (coordinator.py:173-184).
- B12: Race: a spec deleted while a pass is suspended in a save is still processed from the pass's stale list and its tasks are re-created; no sweep removes tasks whose spec is gone and they are deletion_protected (Home Keeper's own entry is loaded), so they become undeletable zombies (declarative_companion_sync.py:334, store.py:1799-1830).
- B12: A spec that grows past the 500-match cap after saving silently stops reconciling (only _LOGGER.exception with a traceback on every pass); tasks of entities removed later stay forever and cannot be deleted (declarative_companion_sync.py:344-350).
- B12: exclude_entity_ids is keyed by entity_id while tasks are keyed by registry id, so renaming an excluded entity makes a task appear for it (declarative_companions.py:436).
- B12: Removing a notes template leaves the last rendered notes (e.g. an old reading) on every task forever, now unlocked (owns_notes, declarative_companions.py:582-590).
- B13: percent_low emits a unit-blind threshold (state < 10) while life_low checks the unit; HA tuya robot-vacuum keys side_brush_life/rolling_brush_life/duster_cloth_life declare no unit (DP unit is used), so a model reporting minutes left would only fire under 10 minutes (unverified: needs the Tuya sd DP spec)
- B13: _TIME_FACTORS has plural and abbreviated forms but no singular 'hour', 'minute', 'second' or 'secs': life_low never fires for such a unit, and wear_high compares a 'minute' counter as hours (60x early)
- B13: preset_by_id is defined and documented as used by the WS endpoint and store, but nothing calls it (dead helper; docstring is wrong)
- B14: Any exception inside async_evaluate (KeyError from trigger_task/complete_task/set_sensor_baseline on a task deleted during a suspended pass, or TypeError from a naive stored timestamp in latest_decision_ts/backstop_due) aborts the whole pass and, on the coordinator path (coordinator.py:215-216), fails the refresh so every Home Keeper entity goes unavailable.
- B14: After unload, _tracked is not reset and nothing stops a pass that starts late (an in-flight coordinator refresh): _resubscribe_state can then register a state listener that no unload callback removes, and that pass acts through the replaced store object.
- B14: clear_on_recover has no hold or hysteresis on the recovery side: with for_seconds 0 a reading jittering around the limit records a real completion plus a triggered event on every flap.
- B14: An unknown sensor mode is baselined as usage (holds_edge_state / async_baseline else-branch) but evaluated as threshold (async_evaluate else-branch -> KeyError on cfg['comparison']); reachable only through a downgrade or hand-edited storage.
- B15: home_keeper.adjust_part_stock delta 'nan' (vol.Coerce(float)) sets stock to 0 and fires out-of-stock, because max(0.0, nan) returns 0.0 (assets.py:1368; out of slice scope)
- B15: Next-due sensor copies uncapped completion note text into state attributes; a long note pushes attributes past the recorder's 16 KB limit, so they are dropped with a warning (sensor.py:215-217, models.normalize_completion_metadata has no note cap)
- B15: Deleting a foreign device makes HA remove the linked per-task entities; they only come back on the next reload, on a self-owned fallback device, with the unknown device_id warning logged on every setup (coordinator.py:399-418, no device-registry listener)
- B15: Count sensor runs effective_filter_tasks + count_tasks twice per state write (native_value and extra_state_attributes) with two different now values (sensor.py:326-332)
- B16: Task names go into title/message of legacy notify services whose schema is cv.template, so Jinja in a name is rendered and an invalid one ('{{' or '{#' unclosed) fails validation and the push is dropped at debug level (notifier.py:151)
- B16: Android shows at most 3 action buttons; a notification configured with all 4 verbs silently loses the last one on Android phones
- B16: persistent_notification sends carry no data.notification_id, so repeated sends stack in the HA notification drawer instead of replacing
- B16: A Snooze tap uses the notification's current snooze_hours, not the hours printed on the button it was built with
- B16: profiles.matches_filter docstring still points to notifications.is_walkable, which no longer exists
- B17: tag_listener schedules completions with hass.async_create_task, not an entry-scoped task; a completion racing an entry reload writes through the old store instance and can be overwritten by the new one
- B17: tag_listener _complete catches only KeyError/TaskValidationError; any other exception aborts the remaining matched tasks and skips async_settle_buy_tasks
- B17: Unverified: a 2026.9 ChildDeviceEntry may not expose config_entries, which _split_successor/_resolve_by_snapshot read (devices.py:230, 584); heal would raise (caught by setup)
- B17: Unverified: removing our config entry from a device (detach_legacy/prune) may make HA's entity registry remove our entity-linked entities on it
- B18: Problem sync reloads the whole entry for every created/deleted mirror even when it owns no per-task entities (device-less template sensors), unlike reconcile_buy_tasks and the declarative reconciler (store.py:1687-1696)
- B18: _task_name treats a missing state as authoritative at startup: mirrors are renamed to the registry original_name (e.g. 'Problem') and back once states load, costing two saves and a transient wrong name per restart (problem_sync.py:151-158)
- B18: Each problem-sensor flap records a completion and rewrites the whole store with no debounce; a chatty sensor causes heavy disk writes and event spam (problem_sync.py:179-182)
- B18: The one-off purge in the setup's first refresh schedules a redundant reload that runs right after setup completes (coordinator.py:299-308)
- B18: _problem_notes entries for entity ids that no longer exist are never pruned (store.py:480-486)
- B18: Problem-mirror completion history is dropped on orphan deletion (sync toggled, sensor excluded) with no archiving, unlike part/buy tasks
- B19: Options flow re-validates stored values with stricter selectors than the panel or service accept (retention > 3650, a non-binary_sensor exclusion entity, Home Keeper's own todo as shopping target), so the Configure dialog fails validation on a field the user never touched (config_flow.py:92-117)
- B19: set_options accepts allow_snooze/allow_skip/allow_due_today/profiles/notifications, but services.yaml and strings.json omit them, so the action editor and the generated API reference do not show them (__init__.py:761-792 vs services.yaml:1404-1486)
- B19: normalize_profiles does not de-duplicate ids: two profiles with one id give duplicate count-sensor unique_ids (second entity dropped) and collide in todo_list desired/by_id maps (profiles.py:129-133)
- B19: A hand-written set_options that re-sends a synced profile without its id is guarded only against notifications; its sync items are removed and re-added and its count sensor is recreated with a new unique_id (options.py:263-306)
- B19: An options-flow save made while a panel/service save's reload is in flight skips its reload (caller_is_reloading is entry-wide), so the flow change can miss the setup that is already running (options.py:125-130, __init__.py:973)
- B20: quality_scale docs-removal-instructions says 'README covers removal', but neither README.md nor docs/guide has removal steps, and async_remove_entry deletes all tasks, appliances and uploaded files without any documented warning.
- B20: quality_scale test-coverage is claimed done, but diagnostics.py has no test in any tier (nothing under tests/ mentions diagnostics), which is how B20-1 and B20-2 went unnoticed.
- B20: hacs.json declares no minimum Home Assistant version, yet card.py imports lovelace.const.LOVELACE_DATA at module load and reads LovelaceData.resource_mode; on an older HA the import fails for the whole integration, and a missing resource_mode makes _sync raise with no fallback delivery.
- B20: Language change: between EVENT_CORE_CONFIG_UPDATE and the reload's preload, and whenever the entry is not loaded (listener gone, websocket commands still registered), resolve_exception/resolve_string read the new language's tables on the loop.
- B20: template_context.template_variables injects area_id, area_name and device_id as values, which shadow HA's template functions of the same names; '{{ area_name(entity_id) }}' fails with 'not callable', while __init__.py:1098-1100 cites area_id as a reachable registry helper.
- B21: update_asset accepts `kind` and `source` (via _ASSET_FIELDS) but assets.merge_update silently ignores both - asset twin of the already-reported update_task `source` issue (__init__.py:481-503, assets.py:1585-1638).
- B21: trigger_task: strings.json:314 says 'Only valid for triggered tasks' but store.trigger_task accepts sensor tasks too; and api_surface EVENT_TASK_TRIGGERED summary says 'moving from dormant to due-now' while re-arming an already-armed task re-fires the event and resets next_due to now (store.py:616-627).
- B21: add_declarative_companion declares no fields in services.yaml and its translated description (strings.json/en.json, which override services.yaml) drops the payload shape, so neither the HA action editor nor the generated API reference says what the required spec fields are.
- B21: delete_declarative_companion service (and ws twin) discard store.async_delete_declarative_companion's entity_set_changed result and never reload, so per-task entities of the removed managed tasks may linger until the next reload (needs confirmation that declarative tasks own entities).
- B21: Schema/selector gaps beyond retention: snooze_task hours has no max in the schema (selector max 8760) so a huge value raises OverflowError in the handler; vol.Coerce(float) fields (delta, baseline, cost, reading) accept 'nan'/'inf' via YAML, and assets._round_stock(int(inf/nan)) then raises.
- F01: History trash icons delete a completion or skip on one tap with no confirmation (panel-history.ts:338-347), unlike task and appliance deletes; deleting the latest completion also rolls back next_due and the meter baseline.
- F01: `_reload` sends `config_entries/get` twice per refresh (getEntryDomains and getLoadedEntryIds), a redundant full config-entry serialization on every panel action (panel.ts:783-784).
- F01: `set hass` switches the i18n language but does not re-render, so a profile language change shows mixed-language UI until the next render (panel.ts:295).
- F01: `openProfile` rebuilds the Profiles host out of band and pushes new ha-forms into `_liveHassEls` without dropping the detached ones; every hass update keeps re-rendering dead forms until the next full render (panel-settings.ts:1612-1616).
- F01: `parseRoute` calls `decodeURIComponent` unguarded; a hand-typed malformed `%` in the URL throws out of `set route` before HA appends the element, leaving a blank page (utils.ts:1240-1272, panel.ts:311-314).
- F02: Duplicate relinks the consumable without the original task's per-task source.part.quantity (setTaskConsumable is called without quantity), so the copy draws the part default.
- F02: Switching an existing threshold/state task to availability mode keeps the adopted sensor_clear_on_recover=false, so the availability default of auto-clear on is lost (form shows it unchecked, so WYSIWYG).
- F02: Clearing the recurrence_type dropdown sends recurrence_type '' and the edit fails with 'invalid recurrence_type' (visible error).
- F03: Delete-profile confirm/blocked dialog names the render-time profile.name (closure at :1158/:1353/:1360), so after an in-place rename it shows the old name
- F03: Profile delete pre-check reads p._options only; a notification whose profile picker was just switched to that profile (debounce still armed, :1490) passes the check, then its save stores a dangling profile_id the backend accepts (options.py:276-283), and the notification silently sends nothing
- F04: resolveSnoozePreset '1h' uses setHours, so from the first 01:xx of a DST fall-back it snoozes 2 real hours (utils.ts:1347; verified TZ=America/Los_Angeles).
- F04: forms.ts:2226 formats the part preview's first-due date with formatDate(first) and no lang, so it uses the browser locale, not HA's language.
- F04: dueLabel's meter countdown (utils.ts:935) and tn()'s {n} (i18n.ts:106) print raw numbers with no locale decimal mark or grouping, unlike formatReading.
- F04: isSafeImageUrl (utils.ts:89-91) claims to reject protocol-relative URLs, but '/\\host/x' and '/\t/host' pass and resolve off-host; harmless today because http(s) URLs are allowed anyway.
- F05: hide_when_empty hides via this.style.display='none' and fires no card-visibility-changed, so in sections view the hui-card grid cell (rows: 4) likely stays as a blank gap (not verified against HA source).
- F05: _stateSignal keys on entity ids containing 'home_keeper': renaming todo.home_keeper_tasks / calendar.home_keeper_upcoming_tasks stops the live refresh, and unrelated entities with that substring trigger refreshes (card.ts:513-514).
- F05: A background refresh rebuilds the open '+ Add task' ha-form (innerHTML replace) and drops focus mid-typing (card.ts:770, 1201).
- F05: sort: area puts tasks with no area first (areaName '' sorts first), while group_by: area sinks them last (card-filter.ts:588-593).
- F05: The card calls home_keeper/get_options (not admin-gated; returns every option and notify targets) as any user just to read three deferral switches (card.ts:561; websocket_api.py:1178).
- F06: Key editor rows still being filled in (name typed, key blank or a repeat) are lost on any dialog re-render (trigger-mode change, failed save), since renderKeyEditor rebuilds from the draft (panel-declarative.ts:768-771, 904-905, 410-411).
- F06: ensureIntegrations caches [] forever after one failed fetch, and the preset list with its match counts is cached for the panel's life, so the integration dropdown and the 'matches your entities' grouping go stale (panel-declarative.ts:347-365).
- F06: saveDeclarative: if _refresh() rejects after a successful save, the error is written to the already-closed dialog state and never shown (panel-declarative.ts:407-412).
- F07: Appliance detail sub-tab nav aria-label uses asset.name, blank for an unnamed existing-device appliance (panel-detail.ts:611).
- F07: Part 'Replaced {date}' chip prints the raw stored date, not formatDate in the panel language (panel-detail.ts:735).
- F07: cleanupOrphans stops at the first failed delete and leaves later orphans untouched, with a raw backend message (panel-lists.ts:151-155).
- F07: assetAncestry falls back to the raw asset id for a blank-named parent; reachable only through the service API since the picker offers virtual parents only (panel-lists.ts:412).
- F07: areaChip shows the raw area id for an area no longer in the registry, contrary to its doc comment, because areaName falls back to the id (panel-chips.ts:107-112).
- F07: Detail page shows 'X is no longer installed. You can safely delete this task here.' for an owner that is only in setup_retry (panel-detail.ts:387-389).
- F08: Upload URLs put asset and part ids into the path without encodeURIComponent (api.ts:759, 787), and the backend accepts caller-chosen part ids verbatim (assets.py:651). A part id that contains '#', '?', '/' or '%' can never receive a file.
- F08: The openDocument fallback calls window.open(doc.url) without the isHttpUrl check that the anchor path uses (documents.ts:238-247). Only unsaved local drafts can reach it, because the backend validates stored URLs.
- F08: There is no client-side check of the 50-document limit (assets.py:117). At the limit, an upload sends up to 100 MB before the server rejects it.
- F08: addLinkDocument on an unsaved appliance accepts any URL text. The bad link makes the whole Create fail later, and the error appears only in the bottom banner.
- F09: home_keeper_task_skip_updated fired by move_skip carries only the old ts and no new ts (store.py:2383-2386), so a listener mirroring skips cannot follow the move; EVENTS.md:62-69 says the event's ts identifies the entry for update/move/delete_skip.
- F09: ws get_options (websocket_api.py:1178) is open to every signed-in user and returns notification targets, profiles and problem-sensor exclusions plus notify_targets, while the card only needs allow_snooze/allow_skip/allow_due_today; set_options is admin-only.
- F09: EVENTS.md:99-106 says clear_on_recover applies to threshold or state bindings and the auto-completion consumes one spare; models.py:371-431 accepts it on all four edge modes (availability defaults it on) and the draw is consume_quantity/link quantity.
- F10: 1h snooze preset uses wall-clock setHours, so at 01:xx on the DST fall-back day it snoozes 2 real hours (utils.ts:1347); the backend `hours` service has the same wall-clock semantic, so it is consistent but wrong for both.
- F10: Move-date dialog: clearing the date and pressing Save does nothing and shows no error (panel-dialogs.ts:85 `if (!p._hass || !m.task || !m.newTs) return;`).
- X01: Declarative-companion services (add/update/delete) do not translate KeyError/TaskValidationError into ServiceValidationError, so an unknown id or bad spec surfaces as a raw unknown_error, unlike the websocket twins (__init__.py:1866-1893).
- X01: home_keeper/get_options is open to non-admins and returns every saved notification (targets, schedules), profiles and notify_targets although the Settings UI is admin-only (websocket_api.py:1178-1201).
- X01: delete_archived_completion silently succeeds when task_id/ts match nothing, and the service resolves task_id against live tasks by name, so a name that matches a live task deletes nothing without an error (__init__.py:1277-1285, store.py:2399-2409).
- X02: Periodic refresh runs as an entry background task; _purge_expired_one_offs (coordinator.py:306) starts a reload from inside it, and the unload cancels that refresh at its next await. An overdue/due-soon transition detected in the same tick has already persisted its edge state, so the async_send_auto push is lost for good.
- X02: find_coordinator (coordinator.py:421) ignores entry.state. HA keeps runtime_data after a setup failure that comes after __init__.py:830, so services, websocket commands and the HTTP views keep using a coordinator that has shut down. This contradicts async_unload_entry's reliance on integration_not_loaded.
- X02: async_remove_entry can be undone by in-flight untracked work: a late _save() recreates .storage/home_keeper after store.async_remove(), and an upload's _move recreates the documents tree after rmtree. A reinstall then brings back the removed data.
- X02: To-do list sync bails on _stopped after _apply without persisting bookkeeping for writes that already landed. With vanish_as_completed, a line that a stale pass removed after a retarget reads as vanished in the next pass, which gives a spurious task completion (narrow race).
- X02: SensorTaskWatcher._handle_state_change starts a full evaluation of every sensor task (with template renders) for each state change of any bound entity. Nothing coalesces these runs and there is no re-entrancy guard, unlike TodoSyncDriver, so a 1 Hz meter with many companion tasks gives O(N) work per event.
- X03: Import does not remap card_links asset ids when an appliance id is re-minted (match none, non-uuid ids), so copied tasks link to the original appliance or to nothing
- X03: One-off purge deletes tasks from a list computed before its awaits; a completion undone meanwhile is still deleted (coordinator._purge_expired_one_offs)
- X03: delete of a declarative-companion spec or reconciler-removed declarative/problem tasks drops completion history that delete_task would archive onto an appliance
- X03: _mutate_asset fires asset_updated with the stale asset object after an await in remove_asset_document/remove_part_file if the asset was replaced or deleted meanwhile
- X04: Card filter 'today' still includes auto-buy reminders although 'overdue' was changed to exclude them (card-filter.ts:506-508).
- X04: Snooze presets and the snooze_task hours field count from now, so snoozing a task that is not due yet moves it earlier, contrary to 'Snooze moves the due date later' (defer.ts:179-180, __init__.py:1361).
- X04: set_due_today writes next_due = now, so the task is Overdue at once and auto 'overdue' notifications fire right after the user asked for 'today' (store.py:719).
- X04: Transitions that happen while HA is down are silently baselined on restart, so an overdue or due-soon notification for a task that fell due during downtime never sends (coordinator.py:235-239).
- X04: A runtime change of HA's time zone keeps anchor instants rather than wall clock, so every fixed schedule moves to a different local hour and possibly day (recurrence.py:271).
- X06: docs-preview.yml paths filter: a PR whose later push removes every doc-path change no longer matches on `closed`, so its pr-preview stays on gh-pages.
- X06: preview-release.yml: when a PR's manifest X.Y.Z changes (a rebase across a stable), the replace step deletes only the new tag, so the old vX.Y.Z.devN release stays listed until the PR closes.
- X06: release.yml has no concurrency group: two merges at the same version race on `git push origin vX`, and one push-to-main run goes red.
- X06: release-issues.py _CLOSES reads only the first number in 'Fixes #12, #13' and misses 'Fixes: #12', so #13 never closes. The --missing cross-check sees it only when a commit names it.
- X06: notify-issues comments on an issue that is already closed, and on a stable it re-closes the issue with state_reason completed, which overwrites 'not planned'. On a beta, the comment says 'This issue stays open' on a closed issue.
- X06: build-zip.sh `-x package.json`/`tsconfig.json`/`rollup.config.mjs` match only at the zip root, so frontend/package.json, package-lock.json, tsconfig.json and rollup.config.mjs ship in home_keeper.zip.
- X06: setup-ci-deps.sh lock: a second run can read an empty pid file between mkdir and echo, treat the lock as stale and run concurrently. The first run's EXIT trap then deletes the second run's lock.
- X06: notify-discord.yml has no permissions block and calls a reusable workflow pinned to @main of another repository on issue_comment/issues, which any GitHub user can trigger. The log shows the reusable workflow passes the text through env vars.
- X06: test.yml and hacs.yml run hacs/action@main and home-assistant/actions/hassfest@master unpinned, with the default token permissions.
- X07: No test attaches a Home Keeper device trigger and fires its event; only device_automation/trigger/list is asserted (tests/integration/test_events.py:584), so drift between device_trigger._attach_filter keys and the events.py payload keys would make every device-trigger automation silently never fire
- X07: ha_stubs parse_datetime returns None for None/non-str input, but real HA raises TypeError; no live call site passes None today (anchor is required), but the stub would mask one
- X07: FakeSyncStore.complete_task pops the task, but the real store keeps a recurring task with a new next_due, so the sync-driver suites never exercise the 'ticked task is still desired' next pass
- X07: FakeTodoHass.async_create_task closes the coroutine unrun, so the state-change and options-change forced passes are never executed in unit suites (integration covers some)
- X08: reconcile_buy_tasks / reconcile_problem_sensor_tasks replace store._tasks with a new dict, so coordinator.data still points at the old map for the async_update_listeners() write that async_settle_buy_tasks makes before the refresh (coordinator.py:194, store.py:1610)
- X08: Asset list rows use p._assets.filter/find per row (panel-lists.ts:367, 401, 410): O(A^2), negligible at 100 appliances
- X09: todo.py:127 checking off a to-do item whose task was deleted between the coordinator refresh and the tap lets store.complete_task's raw KeyError escape (only TaskValidationError is caught), so the card shows an unlocalized 'Unknown error'.
- X09: notifier.py:376 notify_profile_not_found for a saved notification with a dangling profile passes the profile id (uuid) into 'No notification profile named {profile}', so the message names a uuid, not a name.
- X10: Removing a foreign device (e.g. a ZHA device) leaves tasks pointing at the dead device_id with no listener to detach or reload; HA unlinks the linked per-task entities (device_id=None) until the next reload, which then mints one self-owned 'Maintenance task' device per task and logs the unknown-device warning on every reload (coordinator.py:411-427).
- X10: sensor.py:115-118 checks the task '_next_due' branch before the asset-date branch, so a tracked metadata entry whose service-supplied id ends in '_next_due' has its registry entry pruned and recreated on every reload, losing entity customizations.
- X11: Per-row icon buttons in the card and the history list all share one label ('Done', 'Snooze', 'Delete', 'Edit') with no task or date in the accessible name, so a screen-reader button list reads 'Done, Done, Done'.
- X11: Chip overflow toggle (panel-lists.ts:313) has an accessible name of '+3' or a bare minus sign; 'Show all' is only a title.
- X12: Stock box sends typed absolute value as a delta against the render-time `committed` stock (panel-detail.ts:936-949); if the stock moved since render (completion elsewhere; panel never refreshes) the stored count is not the typed one (toast shows real value).
- X12: cleanupOrphans has no in-flight guard (panel-lists.ts:147-157): a double-click deletes each task a second time and shows a not_found error toast.
- X12: submitMoveCompletion double-click: second call fails not_found and writes the error into the already-detached _moveCompletion object, so it is never shown (panel-dialogs.ts:83-95).
- X12: testNotification assigns p._options from its set_options answer without the claimSave/isNewest guard (panel-settings.ts:1709), so a stale answer can overwrite a newer list write.
